- **che-context-optimize**: Shrink an oversized always-loaded context file (global ~/.claude/CLAUDE.md, a project CLAUDE.md, or an auto-memory MEMORY.md index) WITHOUT dropping any rule. Use when the 40k warning fires, when the harness says "MEMORY.md is over the read limit / only part of it was loaded / compact it now", when docs-health reports an oversized context file, or when the user says "optimize CLAUDE.md", "context file too big", "shrink memory". Enforces per-item before→after reconciliation (not sampling) so nothing is silently lost.
